# JWW Header Field Matrix

Reference: https://www.jwcad.net/jwdatafmt.txt (Version 7.02, internal v600/v700).

Generated by `node tools/jww-format-field-matrix.mjs`. Companion: [gap audit](JWW_FORMAT_GAPS_AUDIT.md).
Offsets refer only to the built-in template with its original CString lengths; they are not universal offsets.
All rows are parsed into `meta.jwwInternalSettings.document.fields` and serialized with source-byte retention for unchanged values.
`formatAudit.test.js` covers every descriptor's primitive round trip. This does not establish native application semantics for every value.
`dummyBeforeSunpou` and `dummyAfterSunpou` are Gateway names for the unnamed serialized dummy fields.

Fields: 3221; template header bytes: 15984.
